# GlyphVault puzzle investigations archive

Historical operator notes retained for audit. Normative behavior lives in `docs/policy/`, `docs/errata/`, and `docs/puzzle_handbook.md`.

## INC-2020-0000

Postmortem notes for GlyphVault dungeon regression review. Timeline summary: operators at Glyph Atrium compared Terraform-seeded clue rows with PNG atlas tiles and found base64 clue_blob decoding drifted from handbook guidance. Attachment 000000019919 archives redacted solver transcripts and sqlite snapshots.

## REV-2020-0001

Instructor Blake facilitated a tabletop at Terraform Vault about one-based atlas tile coordinates. Trainees were asked to read errata before touching the C analysis engine. The cohort kept discussion qualitative and deferred code changes until policy binders were aligned.

## AUD-2020-0002

Audit trail excerpt from SQLite Gallery. Reviewer Casey noted that puzzle replay batch 2 still queried legacy clues tables during glyph render. Corrective theme: room_clues.clue_blob must be decoded before atlas sampling. Evidence packet 00000001d6f7 includes annotated handbook pages only.

## OPS-2020-0003

Operator journal from Atlas Mezzanine. On shift 3, Devon rehearsed room_exits requires_key gating while mentoring a new hire. The trainee initially trusted a decoy shell script; the mentor redirected them to puzzle-analyze and the Terraform fixture workflow.

Postmortem notes for GlyphVault dungeon regression review.
